      """Render the same Markdown to an editable Word file and a bounded review PDF."""
      from pathlib import Path
      import csv,json,re,html,sys
      HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
      sys.path.insert(0,str(HERE.parents[1]/'.venv/lib/python3.14/site-packages'))
      from docx import Document
      from docx.shared import Inches,Pt
      from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH
      from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate,Paragraph,Spacer,Image,Table,TableStyle,KeepTogether
      from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle
      from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_JUSTIFY,TA_CENTER,TA_LEFT
      from reportlab.lib import colors
      from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
      from PIL import Image as PILImage
      import pymupdf

      def render():
          source=(HERE/'manuscript.md').read_text()
          with (HERE/'figures/residual-contour-areas.csv').open() as f: rr=list(csv.DictReader(f))
          groups=list(dict.fromkeys(r['group'] for r in rr))
          table=[['Model / terminal run','50% (km²)','95% (km²)','99% (km²)']]
          for g in groups:
              label=('OpenAP 2.6' if '--openap26-' in g else '2020 polar')+' / '+('A' if g.startswith('37097311') else 'B')
              values=[f"{float(r['grid_area_km2']):,.0f}" for r in rr if r['group']==g]
              table.append([label]+values)
          mdtable='Table 1. Residual display-region areas for Figure 2; cowling repeat A. Areas are not calibrated search guarantees.\n\n'
          mdtable+='\n'.join('| '+' | '.join(row)+' |' for row in [table[0],['---']*4]+table[1:])
          source=source.replace('{{CONTOUR_TABLE}}',mdtable)
          (HERE/'MH370-review-manuscript.md').write_text(source)
          styles={
            'body':ParagraphStyle('body',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=12,leading=13.8,alignment=TA_JUSTIFY,spaceAfter=0),
            'heading':ParagraphStyle('heading',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=12,leading=14.4,spaceBefore=10,spaceAfter=5,keepWithNext=True),
            'title':ParagraphStyle('title',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=16,leading=19,alignment=TA_CENTER,spaceAfter=9),
            'caption':ParagraphStyle('caption',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=11,leading=12.6,spaceAfter=7),
            'table':ParagraphStyle('table',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=11,leading=12.5),
            'eq':ParagraphStyle('eq',fontName='Times-Roman',fontSize=12,leading=15,alignment=TA_CENTER,spaceBefore=6,spaceAfter=6)}
          doc=Document();sec=doc.sections[0]
          sec.page_width=Inches(8.2677);sec.page_height=Inches(11.6929)
          sec.top_margin=sec.bottom_margin=sec.left_margin=sec.right_margin=Inches(1)
          normal=doc.styles['Normal'];normal.font.name='Times New Roman';normal.font.size=Pt(12)
          normal.paragraph_format.line_spacing=1;normal.paragraph_format.space_after=Pt(0);normal.paragraph_format.space_before=Pt(0)
          story=[]; blocks=re.split(r'\n\s*\n',source.strip());i=0
          while i<len(blocks):
              b=blocks[i].strip()
              if b.startswith('!['):
                  path=HERE/re.search(r'\]\((.*?)\)',b).group(1)
                  w,h=PILImage.open(path).size;ww=451.27;hh=ww*h/w
                  if hh>475: ww*=475/hh;hh=475
                  im=Image(str(path),width=ww,height=hh)
                  caption=blocks[i+1].strip() if i+1<len(blocks) and blocks[i+1].startswith('Figure ') else ''
                  story.append(KeepTogether([Spacer(1,7),im,Paragraph(escape(caption),styles['caption'])]))
                  p=doc.add_paragraph();p.alignment=WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER;p.add_run().add_picture(str(path),width=Inches(ww/72));p.paragraph_format.keep_with_next=True
                  if caption:doc.add_paragraph(caption);i+=1
              elif b.startswith('|'):
                  data=[[Paragraph(escape(c),styles['table']) for c in row] for row in table]
                  t=Table(data,colWidths=[190,87,87,87]);t.setStyle(TableStyle([('LINEABOVE',(0,0),(-1,0),.6,colors.black),('LINEBELOW',(0,0),(-1,0),.4,colors.black),('LINEBELOW',(0,-1),(-1,-1),.6,colors.black),('TOPPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),4),('BOTTOMPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),4)]));story.extend([t,Spacer(1,7)])
                  dt=doc.add_table(rows=len(table),cols=4);dt.style='Table Grid'
                  for row,cells in zip(table,dt.rows):
                      for s,c in zip(row,cells.cells):c.text=s
              elif b.startswith('#'):
                  level=len(b)-len(b.lstrip('#'));text=b[level:].strip();sty='title' if level==1 else 'heading'
                  story.append(Paragraph(escape(text),styles[sty]));p=doc.add_paragraph(text);p.paragraph_format.keep_with_next=True;p.paragraph_format.space_before=Pt(10);p.paragraph_format.space_after=Pt(5)
                  if level==1:p.alignment=WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER;p.runs[0].font.size=Pt(16)
              elif b.startswith('Z_m'):
                  text='Z_m = ∫ q_m(x) L_d(x) L_s(x) dx;\na′_m = a_m Z_m / Σ_k(a_k Z_k);\np_res(x) = Σ_m[a_m q_m(x) L_d(x) L_s(x)] / Σ_k(a_k Z_k).  (1)'
                  # Unicode maths in Word remains editable; typeset PDF uses explicit mathematical symbols.
                  story.append(Paragraph('Z<sub>m</sub> = ∫ q<sub>m</sub>(x)L<sub>d</sub>(x)L<sub>s</sub>(x) dx<br/>a′<sub>m</sub> = a<sub>m</sub>Z<sub>m</sub> / Σ<sub>k</sub>(a<sub>k</sub>Z<sub>k</sub>)<br/>p<sub>res</sub>(x) = Σ<sub>m</sub>[a<sub>m</sub>q<sub>m</sub>(x)L<sub>d</sub>(x)L<sub>s</sub>(x)] / Σ<sub>k</sub>(a<sub>k</sub>Z<sub>k</sub>).  (1)',styles['eq']))
                  doc.add_paragraph(text)
              else:
                  txt=' '.join(b.splitlines());sty='caption' if txt.startswith('Table 1.') else 'body'
                  story.append(Paragraph(escape(txt),styles[sty]));story.append(Spacer(1,4))
                  p=doc.add_paragraph(txt);p.alignment=WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.JUSTIFY
              i+=1
          def page(c,d):
              c.setFont('Times-Roman',9);c.drawString(72,30,'MH370 — research-review draft — 18 September 2026');c.drawRightString(A4[0]-72,30,str(d.page))
          pdf=HERE/'MH370-review-manuscript.pdf'
          SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf),pagesize=A4,rightMargin=72,leftMargin=72,topMargin=72,bottomMargin=72,title='Conditional evidence and false precision in MH370 reconstruction',author='Pete Large').build(story,onFirstPage=page,onLaterPages=page)
          doc.save(HERE/'MH370-review-manuscript.docx')
          d=pymupdf.open(pdf)
          metrics={'pages':len(d),'words_including_references_captions_table':len(re.findall(r"\b[\w]+(?:[’'-][\w]+)*\b",source)),'whitespace_word_count':len(source.split()),'figure_count':source.count('![Figure'),'table_count':1,'pdf_font':'Times-Roman (review-render substitute for Times New Roman)','docx_font':'Times New Roman','font_pt':12,'margin_cm':2.54,'pdf_pages_limit':20,'word_budget':[6000,8000],'no_new_inference':True}
          (HERE/'MANUSCRIPT-CHECKS.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2))
          if len(d)>20:raise ValueError('Manuscript exceeds 20 pages')
          if not 6000<=metrics['words_including_references_captions_table']<=8000:raise ValueError('Word budget failed')
          previews=HERE/'checks';previews.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
          for n in range(len(d)):d[n].get_pixmap(matrix=pymupdf.Matrix(1,1)).save(previews/f'page-{n+1:02}.png')

      def escape(s):return html.escape(s).replace('∑','Σ')
      if __name__=='__main__':render()

      # Review task

      Act as a technically demanding independent reviewer of the supplied MH370 research-review manuscript. Baseline `mh370-review-2026-09-18`. First state which files and tools you can actually access, what can be executed, and what is missing. Do not claim access to Iso threads or live project state.

      Work in three passes, keeping the initial assessments separate:

      1. **Scientific review:** assess novelty relative to primary published work, physical assumptions, observation models, conditional independence, controls, interpretation and accessibility to Journal of Navigation readers. Trace every material quantitative claim to a table, code calculation or located source passage.
      2. **Adversarial review:** try to falsify the strongest conclusions. Find hidden conditioning, evidence counted twice, omitted support, inconsistent time/coordinate conventions, truth leakage, post-selection, dependence, source-version mismatch, and false precision. In particular, try to explain the apparent northern drift/search shift without treating it as established geographic truth.
      3. **Reconciliation:** distinguish verified defects from suspicions, propose decisive bounded tests, and recommend publishable scope. Do not manufacture a reassuring consensus. Return major/minor comments, exact evidence locators, severity, confidence and an actionable proposed correction.

      Priority checks:

      - Reconstruct all four-case mixtures and their model-weight boundary. Does equal weighting after different satellite fits get misrepresented as inferred instrument reliability?
      - Verify common airborne time, R600 BTO correction, exclusion of R1200 BTO, fuel counted once, drift-domain conditioning, and unresolved impact outcomes.
      - Inspect independent terminal and failed synthetic-cruise controls. Are interacting particles or repeat descendants mistaken for independent samples?
      - Recompute 50/95/99% density regions on the documented equal-area grid. Distinguish smoothing/grid sensitivity from statistical uncertainty and detection probability.
      - Verify the same 7,500 km² cells in the independent run. Do not optimise a new region in the evaluation sample or treat a surveyed-area total as a remaining mask.
      - Evaluate debris identification, response, coastal recovery process, rare-event likelihood support and common environmental errors. Test object deletion and alternative transport families.
      - For Pléiades, separate morphology from identity and conditional transport from unconditional evidence. Do not multiply transport alternatives derived from the same images.
      - For hydroacoustics, distinguish plotted vectors from raw triad recordings; repeat full-search null correction; assess edge censoring and periodic interference. Identify what raw data would permit a calibrated likelihood or meaningful non-detection.
      - Check that gain and WSPR controls do not leak truth or overclaim independence. Historical control implementations do not automatically validate the present accident estimator.
      - Check bibliography against full sources, not search snippets. Record exact page/section/table or code line. Flag missing dataset/software citations.
      - Verify the manuscript word/page budget and figure readability. Do not improve precision by deleting uncertainty qualifications.

      Use public web research read-only. Prefer official investigation material, primary technical documentation and peer-reviewed papers. Source disagreement must be reported. Never fabricate a DOI, quote, measurement or computation. Explicitly label a proposed new experiment as proposed; a reasoned expectation is not a result.

      Allow quick deterministic verification and bounded exploratory calculations in a disposable copy. Before heavy computations, state the scientific question, frozen inputs, exact command, expected duration and acceptance criterion. Preserve originals. Do not use this task to launch broad unrestricted inference, overwrite the manuscript of record, contact third parties, publish the package, change Drive permissions or acquire credentials.

      Required deliverables:

      - `review.md`: concise verdict; five most important findings; detailed major/minor comments; defensible novelty; publication recommendation.
      - `claims.csv`: claim ID, manuscript locator, source/artifact locator, verification performed, result, confidence, proposed correction.
      - `reproduction-log.md`: commands actually run, environment, runtime, outputs, checksums and failures; explicitly separate read-only inspection from executed verification.
      - `proposed-revisions.md`: suggested text only, linked to finding IDs; no silent replacement of canonical files.
      - `receipt.json`: baseline ID, input manifest SHA-256, task ID, reviewer/model setting, start/end times, accessible tools, files opened, tests run, unresolved dependencies and human decisions required.

      Success means a traceable, falsifiable assessment, not agreement with the author or a long answer. A missing input should produce a bounded limitation or request, not an invented calculation. If time is limited, prioritise the evidence-admission boundary, mixture reconstruction, failed recovery controls and independent search-region evaluation before prose polishing.

      # MH370 research review — start here

      Baseline: `mh370-review-2026-09-18`. This is a private research-review snapshot, not a journal submission or an approved search recommendation. The dated manifest, not chat memory, defines the material under review. Work after this snapshot must be reviewed separately.

      ## Read first

      1. `MH370-review-manuscript.pdf` for the paper; `.docx` is editable and `.md` is machine-readable.
      2. `REVIEW-INSTRUCTIONS.md` for the review task, required outputs and authority boundary.
      3. `SCIENTIFIC-STATUS.md` for current versus historical findings and the principal failure evidence.
      4. `CLAIM-LEDGER.csv`, `figures/provenance.json`, `figures/residual-contour-areas.csv` and `MANUSCRIPT-CHECKS.json`.
      5. The research and estimator archives for original methods, complete tables and code. `PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json` inventories every included file, its origin, modification time and SHA-256. `EXCLUDED-FILES.csv` states what is not supplied.

      The first three plots are generated from the current **refined** two-run terminal mixture, not the earlier fourfold release. The old one-turn v0.1 is withdrawn.       My recommendation is Astra **Ultra**, if the account offers it, otherwise **Max**.       ## What the package can and cannot reproduce

      The additional archives contain a current canonical source/configuration/input snapshot, source-investigation methods/code/results, refined terminal artifacts, pooled cruise artifacts, and a broad inventory of numerical controls. Large failed/superseded raw trajectory banks, third-party source PDFs, caches, binary executables and credentials are not automatically redistributed.       # Scientific state at the review snapshot

      ## Current versus superseded

      The latest published combined report is `analysis/terminal-refined-mixture/mixed/model-mixture/report/`, generated 15 September 2026. Two 12,288-parent terminal repeats and two aerodynamic models produce eight groups when crossed with two cowling repeats. Each group has twelve evidence/search conditions. The actual canonical source may contain newer numerical developments; ongoing short ancestor-sampling tests reported by the core thread on 18 September have not replaced these maps.

      The old release `runs/mh370/release-v0.1` is explicitly withdrawn. Its August/early-September single-control-mode means and apparent narrow search areas must not be used as current estimates. The earlier broad-flight diagnostic also failed support checks. The review package intentionally retains failure metadata without presenting withdrawn outputs as current science.

      ## Cross-thread synthesis

      | Topic / Iso thread | Current evidence and interpretation | Review emphasis |
      |---|---|---|
      | Core, `thr_rzreetwcyu` | Broad 16/8/8 cruise histories, fuel conditioning, final-contact alternatives, refined impact/drift/search mixtures. Latest finished report 15 September; root README includes subsequent failed proposal experiments. | Long-gap collapse, low effective ancestry, failed fuel-conditioned synthetic recovery, model reliability boundary. |
      | Earlier core, `thr_94ecfzphq2` | Historical integration and release work; one-turn release subsequently withdrawn. | Do not equate historical test passes with current posterior validity. |
      | Publication, `thr_2hjfxguxv4` | Accessible introduction to Bayesian logic, followed by evidence admission, results and uncertainty. Earlier prose fragments are available; no current complete OneDrive Word manuscript was accessible in this session. | This is a new synthesis, not an edit of an authenticated live Word master. |
      | Hydroacoustics, `thr_wkiec86jt2` | Latest expanded timing tests in `.sources/kadri-2024-hydroacoustics/outputs/conditional-hypothesis-tests`, 15 September. Four million source/time draws, whole-search noise controls. No convincing association under both backgrounds. | Raw-array data, filtering, edge censoring, environmental coupling and detection calibration. Older pressure simulations have different coupling priors: do not mix their amplitudes as one result. |
      | WSPR, `thr_e7vf6bjiyt` | Latest truth-separated MH371 rerun and manuscript: 24,000 paths; selected high-score mass worsens error; no demonstrated localisation benefit. | Previously studied truth, dependence, missing historical environmental identities. |
      | Pléiades, `thr_ym8fvgh7s3` | Three conditional transport surfaces and morphology controls. Authoritative likelihood handoff manifest `b16fa3830f607c19e254a9a2b8cc52b05ab79b82028d1ffb31dab10eebee34ba`. | Unidentified objects; models are alternatives, not independent observations; no unconditional update. |
      | Ocean drift, `thr_pexmvwn97e`, `thr_qpqec5d6kr` | Source investigation includes nine/twenty-object, exterior-only, isotope, Australian non-recovery and current-family sensitivities. Current core adds independently repeated cowling surfaces. | Current integrated nine-object condition differs from historical standalone drift atlases. |
      | End of flight, `thr_dvhqyvtdcu` and audits | Public polars, fuel and weather checks; current joint continuation and final-contact model live in core artifacts. | Public approximations, unresolved envelopes, flight-path angle versus attitude; no calibrated water-entry physics. |
      | Antenna gain, `thr_b6qutrkpmh` and historical core controls | Power effect not stably transportable across flight/design controls. Historical MH371 endpoint localisation improves but truth HPD rank worsens. | Historical implementation, few events, ground track versus heading; no early-MH370 blind spatial control. |
      | Search-coverage audits | Detailed ATSB footprint and holes, 50/80/94% effective detection; optional approximate 2018 outline at 60%, dependence alternatives. | Reference masks are not full sonar-quality maps; recent survey total has no inferred invented footprint. |

      ## Critical numbers and boundaries

      - Cruise: 1,020,000 **initial candidates**, not independent posterior samples. Approximately 90.1% at 00:11 lies between 35°S and 38°S under that model.
      - Current residual mixture: approximately 74–83% north of 35°S under nine objects plus ATSB94, across the eight model/repeat combinations. This is sensitivity, not a confidence interval or unconditional wreckage probability.
      - Effective weighted ancestry of residuals: approximately 29–73. Two terminal repeats share the same cruise population.
      - At 7,500 km², cowling repeat A design probabilities 51–62% become 20–22% in the other terminal repeat. With the other cowling repeat included, the full range differs; use the CSV, not an abbreviated claim.
      - Failed fuel-conditioned synthetic pair: 500,000 candidates each, coordinate CDF gaps approximately 64.8/63.2 percentage points. This is not an actual-MH370 result, but blocks a general calibration claim.
      - New review maps: 50/95/99% explicitly calculated; original combined report used 50/90/99%. Display smoothing is 25 km on a 5 km equal-area grid. Original weights define scientific reweighting.
      - Main combined maps exclude antenna, WSPR, Pléiades, hydroacoustic, isotope and Australian non-recovery updates. Some are supported conditional alternatives; absence is not a neutral scientific verdict.

      ## Known manuscript limitations for the reviewer

      The draft is intentionally a research-review document. It does not claim completed physical calibration, full numerical convergence, an optimal search allocation, exhaustive literature coverage or a new detected aircraft observable. It needs a fuller dataset/software bibliography before submission, author declarations, rights review and verified final Word pagination. The supplied PDF uses Times-Roman; Word requests Times New Roman. The shape and timing figures were regenerated from scientific tables, not image-generation tools.

      The review task should challenge these boundaries as well as the calculations. A careful negative result or a recommendation to narrow the paper to methods/controls would be useful. Additional compute should be spent on decisive tests, not on polishing a location conclusion that fails them.

      """Independent, portable arithmetic checks on all supplied impact mixtures.

      This checks saved intermediate quantities; it does not validate upstream
      likelihood physics or reconstruct the original search footprints.
      """
      from pathlib import Path
      import csv,hashlib,json,time
      import numpy as np
      HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

      def main():
          start=time.monotonic();folder=HERE/'evidence/impact-mixture'
          manifest=json.loads((folder/'manifest.json').read_text());maximum=0.;conditions=0;groups=[]
          for entry in manifest['completed']:
              p=folder/Path(entry['path']).name
              assert hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==entry['sha256']
              d=json.loads(p.read_text());base=np.asarray(d['baseline_component_weights'],float)
              native=np.asarray(d['native_drift_support'],bool)
              ell=np.array([-np.inf if v is None else v for v in d['relative_drift_log_likelihood']])
              misses=np.asarray(d['search_miss_likelihoods']);priors=np.array([c['probability'] for c in d['group']['components']]);assert np.all(priors==.25)
              ids=[r['search']['id'] for r in d['results'] if r['evidence']=='flight-only']
              assert np.all((misses>=0)&(misses<=1))
              for r in d['results']:
                  b=base.copy();drift=r['evidence']=='nine-recoveries'
                  if drift:b[:,~native]=0;b/=b.sum(axis=1)[:,None]
                  likelihood=(np.exp(ell) if drift else np.ones(len(ell)))*misses[ids.index(r['search']['id'])]
                  joint=b*likelihood[None,:]*priors[:,None];z=joint.sum();joint/=z
                  errors=[np.max(np.abs(joint.sum(axis=0)-r['mixture_weights'])),np.max(np.abs(joint.sum(axis=1)-r['model_probabilities'])),abs(np.log(z)-r['log_normalizer'])]
                  maximum=max(maximum,*map(float,errors));assert max(errors)<1e-10
                  conditions+=1
              groups.append(entry['id'])
          with (HERE/'figures/residual-contour-areas.csv').open() as f:areas=list(csv.DictReader(f))
          for r in areas:
              z=np.load(HERE/'figures'/(r['group']+'-display-grid.npz'));mass=z['mass'];assert abs(mass.sum()-1)<1e-10
              selected=mass/25>=float(r['density_threshold_per_km2']);p=float(mass[selected].sum())
              assert abs(p-float(r['grid_enclosed_probability']))<1e-10
              assert p>=float(r['probability']) and p-float(r['probability'])<mass.max()+1e-12
              assert selected.sum()*25==float(r['grid_area_km2'])
          checks={'status':'passed','groups':len(groups),'conditional_updates_checked':conditions,'contour_areas_checked':len(areas),'maximum_absolute_arithmetic_difference':maximum,'seconds':time.monotonic()-start,'new_samples':0,'scope':'Saved intermediate arithmetic and contour ranking only; not physical calibration or upstream inference validation.'}
          (HERE/'VERIFICATION.json').write_text(json.dumps(checks,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(checks,indent=2))

      if __name__=='__main__':main()

    ── Created publication/review/package_review.py +107
      """Create versioned, hashed, private review archives without changing sources.

      The inventory explicitly records excluded bulk/third-party/dependency material.
      Archives are split into ordinary independent ZIPs, not opaque multipart ZIPs.
      """
      from pathlib import Path
      import csv,datetime as dt,hashlib,json,os,re,zipfile
      HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
      ROOT=HERE.parents[1]
      OWNER=Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/thr_rzreetwcyu/branching-filter-analysis')
      BASELINE='mh370-review-2026-09-18'
      DENY_PARTS={'.git','.venv','venv','node_modules','__pycache__','target','.cache','cache','caches'}
      SECRET_RE=re.compile(rb'(?:-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----|\b(?:ghp|github_pat)_[A-Za-z0-9_]{30,}|\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,})')
      TEXT={'.md','.txt','.json','.csv','.toml','.py','.rs','.yaml','.yml','.tex','.bib','.js','.ts','.html'}
      rows=[];excluded=[]

      def reason(p,rel,category):
          parts=set(p.parts)
          if parts&DENY_PARTS:return 'cache, dependency installation or build output'
          if p.is_symlink():return 'symlink: target not silently exported'
          if p.name.startswith('.env') or re.search(r'(credential|cookie|token-cache|secret)',p.name,re.I):return 'credential/configuration safety exclusion'
          if p.suffix in {'.pyc','.orig','.rej','.so','.dll','.dylib'}:return 'compiled or patch artifact'
          if category=='research' and ('paper' in p.parts or p.suffix=='.pdf' and not ('outputs' in p.parts or 'output' in p.parts)):
              return 'third-party source publication: retrieve from citation/rights holder'
          if p.suffix in {'.zip','.tgz'}:return 'duplicate packaged archive (expanded scientific files preferred)'
          if category=='analysis':
              r=p.relative_to(OWNER)
              # Full current terminal and pooled data; no raw historical run dump.
              if r.parts[0] in {'terminal-refined-mixture','pooled-arc-density','impact-context-inputs'}:return None
              if p.suffix in {'.md','.toml','.py','.rs','.tex','.bib','.geojson'}:return None
              if p.suffix in {'.png','.svg','.pdf','.html','.npz'}:return None
              if p.suffix=='.json' and p.stat().st_size<4_000_000:return None
              if p.suffix=='.csv' and p.stat().st_size<2_000_000:return None
              if p.name.endswith('.tar.gz'):return None
              return 'bulk historical/raw run or binary; metadata/retrieval identity retained where available'
          if category=='research' and p.stat().st_size>100_000_000:return 'bulk source dataset: use pinned retrieval instructions'
          return None

      def select():
          sets={'code-inputs':[], 'research':[], 'analysis':[]}
          for name in ['crates','configs','inputs']:
              for p in sorted((ROOT/name).rglob('*')):
                  if p.is_file():sets['code-inputs'].append((p,'project/'+str(p.relative_to(ROOT))))
          for name in ['README.md','AGENTS.md','Cargo.toml','Cargo.lock','rust-toolchain.toml']:
              sets['code-inputs'].append((ROOT/name,'project/'+name))
          for p in sorted((ROOT/'.sources').rglob('*')):
              if p.is_file():sets['research'].append((p,'project/'+str(p.relative_to(ROOT))))
          for p in sorted(OWNER.rglob('*')):
              if p.is_file():sets['analysis'].append((p,'analysis/'+str(p.relative_to(OWNER))))
          # Include exact upstream cruise files named by the current pool manifest,
          # even when their numeric rows exceed the general historical-file threshold.
          # They remain explicit exceptions in the inventory, not all old runs.
          for category,items in sets.items():
              kept=[]
              for p,rel in items:
                  why=reason(p,rel,category)
                  if why:excluded.append({'origin':str(p),'bytes':p.stat().st_size,'reason':why});continue
                  kept.append((p,rel))
              sets[category]=kept
          return sets

      def archive(category,items):
          out=HERE/'packages';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
          part=1;z=None;current=None;count=0
          for p,rel in items:
              before=p.stat();data=p.read_bytes()
              if p.suffix in TEXT and SECRET_RE.search(data):
                  excluded.append({'origin':str(p),'bytes':before.st_size,'reason':'high-confidence credential-pattern safety exclusion'});continue
              after=p.stat()
              if (before.st_size,before.st_mtime_ns)!=(after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns):
                  excluded.append({'origin':str(p),'bytes':before.st_size,'reason':'changed during snapshot; not exported'});continue
              if z is None or z.fp.tell()>90_000_000:
                  if z:z.close()
                  current=out/f'MH370-review-{category}-{part:02}.zip';part+=1
                  z=zipfile.ZipFile(current,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=4,allowZip64=True)
              z.writestr(rel,data)
              rows.append({'archive':current.name,'path':rel,'origin':str(p),'modified_utc':dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(before.st_mtime,dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'bytes':len(data),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()})
              count+=1
          if z:z.close()
          print(category,count,'files',part-1,'parts',flush=True)

      def main():
          for category,items in select().items():archive(category,items)
          with (HERE/'EXCLUDED-FILES.csv').open('w') as f:
              w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['origin','bytes','reason']);w.writeheader();w.writerows(excluded)
          manifest={'baseline_id':BASELINE,'created_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'canonical_project':str(ROOT),'authoritative_analysis':str(OWNER),'scope':'Private review snapshot; not a claim of all raw-run dependency closure. Consult excluded-file inventory and source manifests. No inference rerun.','files':rows}
          (HERE/'PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2)+'\n')
          with (HERE/'PACKAGE-INDEX.csv').open('w') as f:
              w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
          # A text-only source digest permits code review without ZIP execution.
          sections=[]
          for p in sorted((ROOT/'crates').rglob('*.rs')):
              sections.append('\n\n## '+str(p.relative_to(ROOT))+'\n\n```rust\n'+p.read_text()+'\n```\n')
          (HERE/'CANONICAL-RUST-SOURCE.md').write_text('# Canonical Rust source snapshot\n\nRead-only review export. File hashes are in PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json.\n'+''.join(sections))
          # Reader pack includes all numeric mixture weights (compress well), not just a narrative.
          reader=[]
          for p in sorted(HERE.rglob('*')):
              if p.is_file() and not any(v in p.relative_to(HERE).parts for v in ['packages','checks','inbox']) and p.name not in ['ARCHIVE-CHECKS.json']:
                  reader.append((p,'review/'+str(p.relative_to(HERE))))
          archive('reader',reader)
          # Reader inventory is separate to avoid a manifest self-hash cycle.
          reader_rows=[r for r in rows if r['archive'].startswith('MH370-review-reader')]
          (HERE/'READER-MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'baseline_id':BASELINE,'files':reader_rows},indent=2)+'\n')
          checks=[]
          for p in sorted((HERE/'packages').glob('*.zip')):
              with zipfile.ZipFile(p) as z:
                  assert z.testzip() is None
                  count=len(z.infolist())
              checks.append({'file':p.name,'bytes':p.stat().st_size,'sha256':hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'entries':count,'zip_crc':'passed'})
          (HERE/'ARCHIVE-CHECKS.json').write_text(json.dumps({'baseline_id':BASELINE,'archives':checks,'excluded_files':len(excluded)},indent=2)+'\n')
          print(json.dumps({'archives':checks,'included_files':len(rows),'excluded_files':len(excluded)},indent=2))

      if __name__=='__main__':main()

      ATSB PDF page 105 › 96 ‹ 
      ATSB – AE-2014-054 
       
       
      Figure 73: Coverage statistics and associated confidence of detection, using 100 m x 100 
      m data gap metric 
       
      Source: ATSB  
      For reference, ‘High Confidence Coverage’ means a >95 per cent confidence of detection; ‘Lower 
      Confidence Coverage’ means on average a 70 per cent confidence of detection, and ‘Data Gaps’ 
      have a zero per cent confidence of detection. 
      For each area defined the final sonar data coverage value is determined by taking the total size of 
      the area and subtracting out each data gap and LPD. Holidays are totaled by area (square 
      kilometres) per individual polygon. This size attribute can be used to filter and further classify data 
      gaps and LPDs.  
      It is worth noting that filtering out all data gaps and LPD areas less than 200 m by 200 m 
      decreases the individual data gap count by 78 per cent, and LPD count by 60 per cent. Using this 
      same metric but filtering by square kilometres reduces the total square kilometre area by 24 per 
      cent and seven per cent respectively. This highlights that many of the data gaps and LPDs are 
      smaller than 200 m by 200 m.  
      Historical analysis has shown that aircraft debris fields typically cover areas larger than 200 m by 
      200 m. Therefore, areas less than 200 m by 200 m can be discounted with a moderate to high 
      degree of confidence as they are not large enough to fully contain the aircraft debris field. 

      ATSB PDF page 123 › 114 ‹ 
      ATSB – AE-2014-054 
       
       
       A range of possible but lower likelihood aircraft impact locations are near 35.2°S 91.9°E on the 
      western side of the 7th arc. 
      The discovery of MH370 debris in 2015 and 2016 has been the sole source of new evidence in 
      the search for MH370 (since March 2014). The analysis of this debris, and particularly the drift 
      studies, have yielded new insights about the likely location of the aircraft. The capture of possible 
      debris in the French satellite imagery only two weeks after the aircraft was lost allows drift 
      modelling results to be refined with considerable accuracy. While it cannot be concluded with 
      certainty that the items identified in the satellite imagery are MH370 debris, the coincidence of the 
      area identified in the drift study with all other search area analysis including the results of the 
      surface and underwater searches is compelling. 
      Hydroacoustic analysis 
      At a depth of about 1,000 m there is a layer of water where sound travel is particularly efficient.  
      This layer is called the Sound Fixing and Ranging Channel, or SOFAR channel. Hydroacoustic 
      monitoring makes use of this channel, and the phenomenon of sound waves being ‘trapped’ in 
      that layer and therefore propagating over a very large distance. 
      As part of the United Nations Comprehensive Nuclear-Test-Ban-Treaty Organization or the 
      Integrated Marine Observing System (IMOS), there are a network of hydrophones installed in 
      oceans around the world. There are two of these stations located in the Indian Ocean; one 
      installed off the coast of Perth (HA01) and one installed at Diego Garcia (HA08). 
      Each station consists of an array of three hydrophones separated in space (Figure 87) and 
      connected to a shore facility via a trunk cable. 
      Figure 87: Diagram of a hydrophone array showing three hydrophones and connection to 
      a shore facility 
       
      Source: Comprehensive Nuclear Test-Ban-Treaty Organization 
      For a given acoustic source origin, the configuration of the three hydrophone produces a different 
      arrival time at each hydrophone. Analysis of these arrival times allows relatively accurate 
      determination of the bearing to the sound source. The array can also give a coarse estimate of the 
      distance from the hydrophone, however the tolerance in these calculations is large. In order to 

      ATSB PDF page 124 › 115 ‹ 
      ATSB – AE-2014-054 
       
       
      accurately identify the origin of a signal, at least two stations are required to perform a 
      triangulation on that signal. 
      After MH370 went missing, it was hypothesised that an impact with the water of a B777 could 
      have created an acoustic source sufficiently large to propagate into the Sound Fixing and Ranging 
      Channel and therefore to the hydrophone stations. As such, the hydroacoustic signals present in 
      the Indian Ocean around the time of the aircraft’s expected impact with water were examined to 
      determine whether they could provide any information to help define the search area. Additional 
      data was also received from recordings of low-frequency underwater acoustic signals from data 
      loggers off the Western Australian coast owned by Curtin University’s Centre for Marine Science 
      and Technology (CMST). 
      The ATSB requested that CMST and DST Group analyse all available signals in an attempt to 
      detect and localise underwater sounds that could be associated with the impact of the aircraft on 
      the water or with the implosion of wreckage as the aircraft sank. 
      The CMST provided two reports to the ATSB: 
       Analysis of Low Frequency Underwater Acoustic Signals Possibly Related to the Loss of 
      Malaysian Airlines Flight MH370, CMST report 2014-30; 23 June 2014, (appendix H). 
       Results of analysis of Scott Reef IMOS underwater sound recorder data for the time of the 
      disappearance of Malaysian Airlines Flight MH370 on 8th March 2014; 4 September 2014, 
      (appendix I). 
      The first report found one acoustic event of interest that occurred at a time that potentially linked it 
      to MH370. This event was received on one of the Integrated Marine Observing System recorders 
      near the Perth Canyon (RCS) and at the Comprehensive Nuclear-Test-Ban-Treaty Organization 
      hydroacoustic station at Cape Leeuwin (HA01). A detailed analysis of these signals resulted in an 
      estimated signal source that was compatible with the timing of the last satellite handshake with the 
      aircraft, but incompatible with the satellite to aircraft range derived from this handshake. 
      The second report used data from a hydroacoustic logger from the Scott Reef to further examine 
      the signal of interest. Based on this further data, the source was identified as being likely of 
      geological origin and originating from the Carlsberg Ridge (source shown in Figure 88 as a red 
      circle). 

      ATSB PDF page 125 › 116 ‹ 
      ATSB – AE-2014-054 
       
       
      Figure 88: Locations of the hydroacoustic stations and the estimated signal source 
      locations 
       
      Source: Google earth, annotated by ATSB using Curtin University Centre for Marine Science and Technology data 
      The Los Alamos National Laboratory in the United States also published an analysis of the 
      hydroacoustic signals (appendix J). The conclusions of this report were that there was a possible 
      candidate arrival at HA01 that indicated the southern portion of the search area. The analysis 
      highlighted that this signal was problematic due to its close proximity in time to the arrival at the 
      station of an acoustic signal from an ice event from Antarctica.  
      CMST, acting as advisor to the ATSB, reviewed all the submitted and published analyses 
      including the Los Alamos National Laboratory report. The result of this review did not identify any 
      further signals of interest.  
      At the first principles review, the complete hydroacoustic data analysis results were presented. 
      Meeting participants determined that hydroacoustic analysis did not contribute any useful new 
      information to the search. 
      Satellite imagery analysis 
      During the initial stages of the search for MH370 there was a concerted effort to identify MH370, 
      or a possible condensation trail from the aircraft, in any available satellite imagery captured over 
      the Malay Peninsula and the Strait of Malacca. Available satellite imagery for the Indian Ocean 
      was also later analysed for the time of the flight and some hours after. The aircraft was not 
      identified in any of this imagery although some possible condensation trails were identified and 
      analysed.      
      During the subsequent surface search in the Indian Ocean, AMSA, with the assistance of the 
      Australian Geospatial-Intelligence Organisation, made requests of foreign governments including 
      France, Italy, Germany, Thailand, the People’s Republic of China and the United States to capture 
      imagery (in various electromagnetic spectra including radar, optical and infrared) using their low 
      earth orbiting satellites in the region of the MH370 search area. The intent was to cover as wide 
      an area as possible with the satellites in the hope that aircraft debris floating on the ocean surface

Does not establish a unique route or our terminal hypotheses. |
      | Davey et al. Applying the common-data requirement to dropped final-contact observations is our inference. |

      Figure 2. Residual 50%, 95% and 99% highest-density display regions. Each panel is a
      separate conditional estimate, not one quarter of a pooled PDF. The 5 km equal-area grid is
      smoothed with a 25 km Gaussian standard deviation. The green outline is the ATSB
      footprint approximation; the seventh arc uses a reference altitude, not every candidate's
      actual altitude. Smoothing can extend shading across a footprint boundary; original particle
      weights determine the search update. These contours are not certified operational search
      limits.
      Table 1. Residual display-region areas for Figure 2; cowling repeat A. Areas are not
      calibrated search guarantees.

    import pathlib,tarfile,re
    root=pathlib.Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/thr_rzreetwcyu/branching-filter-analysis');pattern=re.compile(rb'(?:-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH )?PRIVATE KEY-----|\b(?:ghp|github_pat)_[A-Za-z0-9_]{30,}|\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,})');issues=[];n=0
    for f in root.rglob('*.tar.gz'):
     try:
      with tarfile.open(f,'r:gz') as tf:
       for m in tf:
        if not m.isfile():continue
        n+=1
        if pathlib.Path(m.name).name.startswith('.env'):issues.append((str(f),m.name,'env file'))
        if m.size<10_000_000:
         raw=tf.extractfile(m).read()
         if pattern.search(raw):issues.append((str(f),m.name,'credential pattern'))
     except tarfile.ReadError:issues.append((str(f),'','unreadable'))
    print('nested archive files scanned',n,'issues',issues)

    ── Created publication/review/finalize_review.py +45
      """Finalize the fixed review snapshot and its convenient download index."""
      from pathlib import Path
      import csv,datetime as dt,hashlib,json,zipfile
      import package_review as pack
      HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

      def digest(p):
          h=hashlib.sha256()
          with p.open('rb') as f:
              for b in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''):h.update(b)
          return h.hexdigest()

      def main():
          manifest=json.loads((HERE/'PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json').read_text());known={r['origin'] for r in manifest['files']}
          extra=[(p,'analysis/'+str(p.relative_to(pack.OWNER))) for p in sorted((pack.OWNER/'broader-arc-density').rglob('*')) if p.is_file() and str(p) not in known]
          if extra:
              pack.archive('current-cruise-density',extra)
              manifest['files'].extend(pack.rows)
              (HERE/'PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2)+'\n')
              with (HERE/'PACKAGE-INDEX.csv').open('w') as f:
                  w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(manifest['files'][0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(manifest['files'])
              included={r['origin'] for r in manifest['files']}
              with (HERE/'EXCLUDED-FILES.csv').open() as f:excluded=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['origin'] not in included]
              with (HERE/'EXCLUDED-FILES.csv').open('w') as f:
                  w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['origin','bytes','reason']);w.writeheader();w.writerows(excluded)
              checks=json.loads((HERE/'ARCHIVE-CHECKS.json').read_text())
              for p in sorted((HERE/'packages').glob('MH370-review-current-cruise-density-*.zip')):
                  with zipfile.ZipFile(p) as z:assert z.testzip() is None;count=len(z.infolist())
                  checks['archives'].append(dict(file=p.name,bytes=p.stat().st_size,sha256=digest(p),entries=count,zip_crc='passed'))
              checks['excluded_files']=len(excluded)
              (HERE/'ARCHIVE-CHECKS.json').write_text(json.dumps(checks,indent=2)+'\n')
          md='# MH370 review — downloads\n\nStart with the [review instructions](REVIEW-START.md), [PDF manuscript](MH370-review-manuscript.pdf), [editable Word manuscript](MH370-review-manuscript.docx) and [machine-readable manuscript](MH370-review-manuscript.md).\n\n'
          md+='## Starter pack\n\n[Download the reader pack](packages/MH370-review-reader-01.zip). About 80 MB: paper, figures, current mixture weights, status, claims, citation audit, source digest and verification scripts. The agent should confirm it can unpack it; otherwise upload the plain documents first.\n\n'
          md+='## Full technical supplement\n\nExtract all these ordinary ZIP files into the same folder. They preserve the `project/` and `analysis/` trees. Do not concatenate the ZIP files. The manifest provides each file\'s original path, modification time and SHA-256.\n\n'
          for p in sorted((HERE/'packages').glob('*.zip')):
              if 'reader' not in p.name:md+=f'- [{p.name}](packages/{p.name}) — {p.stat().st_size/1e6:.1f} MB\n'
          md+='\n[Package manifest](PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json) · [File index](PACKAGE-INDEX.csv) · [Excluded files and reasons](EXCLUDED-FILES.csv) · [Archive integrity checks](ARCHIVE-CHECKS.json)\n\nPrivate review only. Google Drive delivery has not occurred; connection and destination are still required. No canonical research or live Word manuscript was overwritten.\n'
          (HERE/'DOWNLOADS.md').write_text(md)
          receipt={'baseline_id':pack.BASELINE,'completed_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'manuscript_checks':json.loads((HERE/'MANUSCRIPT-CHECKS.json').read_text()),'verification':json.loads((HERE/'VERIFICATION.json').read_text()),'technical_manifest_sha256':digest(HERE/'PACKAGE-MANIFEST.json'),'pdf_sha256':digest(HERE/'MH370-review-manuscript.pdf'),'docx_sha256':digest(HERE/'MH370-review-manuscript.docx'),'technical_files':len(manifest['files']),'google_drive':{'uploaded':False,'folder_created':False,'reason':'Google Drive integration is not installed/connected; destination account or parent folder not confirmed.'},'security_checks':{'explicit_credential_filenames_excluded':True,'high_confidence_private_key_and_token_scan':True,'nested_source_archive_members_scanned':12340,'nested_archive_findings':0,'limitations':'Not a proof that every arbitrary secret format or institutional restriction can be detected automatically.'},'source_boundaries':'No raw chat transcripts, credentials, build directories or third-party source-paper PDFs intentionally included. Historical raw model runs are inventoried but many excluded; current figure inputs and current upstream cruise/terminal data are included. External input and physical-calibration limitations remain explicit.'}
          (HERE/'DELIVERY-RECEIPT.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt,indent=2)+'\n')
          pack.rows.clear();pack.refresh_reader()
          # Assert the reader contains exactly the final manuscript and instructions.
          with zipfile.ZipFile(HERE/'packages/MH370-review-reader-01.zip') as z:
              for n in ['MH370-review-manuscript.pdf','MH370-review-manuscript.docx','MH370-review-manuscript.md','REVIEW-START.md','CLAIM-LEDGER.csv','MANUSCRIPT-CHECKS.json','VERIFICATION.json']:
                  assert hashlib.sha256(z.read('review/'+n)).hexdigest()==digest(HERE/n)
          print('Final reader files match; delivery receipt and download index ready.')

      if __name__=='__main__':main()
